# CLAUDE.md

This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.

## Commands

The package manager is **pnpm** (`pnpm-lock.yaml`; there is no `package-lock.json`). Vercel auto-detects it from the lockfile.

```bash
pnpm install       # install dependencies
pnpm dev           # Vite dev server
pnpm build         # production build
pnpm lint          # ESLint (flat config, eslint.config.js)
pnpm preview       # serve the production build locally
```

There is no test suite. Verification is done by running the app against the live Supabase project.

Edge functions MUST be deployed with the explicit project ref (a plain `deploy` targets the wrong project and returns NOT_FOUND):

```bash
supabase functions deploy <name> --project-ref vcmizxflfjcpxeccezlc
```

On the current Windows machine the CLI is not installed and `npx supabase` fails (no win32-x64 binary); deploys, function secrets and live SQL go through the Management API (`https://api.supabase.com/v1/projects/vcmizxflfjcpxeccezlc/...`) with `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN` from `.env.secrets.local`. Cron jobs are created inside Postgres by copying an existing job's command (the `CRON_SECRET` never leaves the database).

## Environment

- `.env.local` — `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` and `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, required at startup ([src/lib/supabase.js](src/lib/supabase.js)).
- Any new `VITE_*` variable must ALSO be added in Vercel project settings and redeployed, or production silently breaks.
- `.env.secrets.local` — `SR_KEY=` (service_role key), read only by the Node maintenance scripts in `scripts/`. Never import it in browser code.
- Hosting is Vercel as an SPA (`vercel.json` rewrites everything to `/`).

## Architecture

React 19 + Vite + Tailwind CSS v4 (via `@tailwindcss/vite`; theme tokens follow Material 3 naming like `bg-primary-container`). No backend server of its own — the app talks directly to Supabase (Postgres + Auth + Storage + Edge Functions). `@` is aliased to `src/`.

### Layout of src/

- `src/pages/*` — thin route wrappers; all routes are declared in [src/App.jsx](src/App.jsx) with route-level `lazy()` code splitting (keep heavy deps like TipTap/JSZip out of the initial bundle).
- `src/features/<feature>/{api,hooks,components,lib}` — the real code, organized by feature (products, syndication, templates, import, media, users, auth, dashboard, activity, search). `api/` modules wrap Supabase queries; `hooks/` wrap them for components.
- `src/components/layout` (AppShell/Sidebar/Topbar) and `src/components/ui` (shared primitives).

### Performance conventions (pass of 2026-09-29 — keep them)

- **Heavy code loads on demand.** The description editor goes through [LazyRichTextEditor](src/components/ui/LazyRichTextEditor.jsx) (TipTap is ~370 kB); ProductDetail's Media / Marketplaces / Aliases tabs, marketplace cards and dialogs are `lazy()` under a local `<Suspense>`; exporters (BulkActionsBar, ProductDetail), the PIM export, the Wix push and Pricing's promo fill libraries (`LIBS` in PricingPage) are `await import()`-ed on click. Never import an exporter or a fill library statically from a page. `vite.config.js` puts React/router and Supabase in vendor chunks — keep the `node_modules/<name>/`-anchored patterns (a loose one pulled @tiptap/react into every page).
- **The API returns at most 1,000 rows per request**, filtered or not. Batch `.in()` reads so one batch can't reach it (`getProducts`, `listMediaFor` in media.js, `imagesForSkus` in bestbuySync.js — a product has at most ~46 media rows), page with `.range()`, or let the database count (`promotion_prices(count)`). Two bugs came from this (Best Buy push got 1–4 of 5 images), and a third on 2026-11-10: Analytics read the first 1,000 of 16 weeks of `kpi_snapshots` (~3,200 rows) and stood three weeks behind (`loadSnapshots` pages now).
- **Image thumbnails are our own**: bucket `product-thumbs` holds `<product-images path>.w200.webp` and `.w480.webp` (square cover crop, [20260929_product_thumbs.sql](supabase/migrations/20260929_product_thumbs.sql)). `getThumbnailUrl` returns them (≤200 px → w200, else w480); `thumbFallback` walks own → weserv (`default=` redirects to the original) → original → placeholder — put `onError={thumbFallback(path)}` on every thumbnail `<img>`. Uploads make them in the browser (`makeThumbnails`), deletions remove them (`deleteStorageObjects`); images added any other way are covered by `node scripts/backfill-thumbnails.mjs` (needs `sharp`, safe to re-run).
- **channel_health** has the `(channel, run_at desc)` index, a daily prune (`channel_health_prune()`, cron `channel-health-prune-daily`: older than 30 days, the latest per channel/target always kept) and `channel_health_item(channel, sku)` for one SKU's item. In the app: `latestSnapshotCounters` for tiles, `latestSnapshotItem` for one SKU, `latestSnapshot` (full `results`) is shared for 5 s — write snapshots with `insertSnapshot` so the next read is fresh. Listing Health and PIM completeness share one catalog load (`loadCatalogWithMedia`), Price Alignment shares its products/promotions reads (`sharedLoad`). The catalog table's minimum width is its fixed columns plus 18rem for Model (`ProductsTable`): a flat 64rem left Model 0 px wide with the stock and MSRP columns on, and on a phone its text ran over Brand (2026-11-10). **The database was the smallest Supabase size (Nano, 0.43 GB RAM) until 2026-10-20**: that day ~25 Listing Health computations in four minutes (nine full channel snapshots + the catalog each, from app reloads) froze it for ~25 minutes until it was restarted (Management API `POST /v1/projects/{ref}/restart`); the user then moved it to **Micro (1 GB)** (`PATCH /v1/projects/{ref}/billing/addons` `{"addon_type":"compute_instance","addon_variant":"ci_micro"}`, ~10 USD/month — the Pro plan's compute credit already covers two other projects). `computeListingHealth()` is shared for 30 s by every caller (React's double effect in development, remounts). Keep heavy reads shared or server-side. The Dashboard's Price Alignment card (next to Catalog, user 2026-10-01; side by side it ends where the Catalog card ends and its channel list scrolls inside — the Catalog sets the row's height, user 2026-10-21) reads the SAME saved reports (`loadLatestAlignment` per target, dynamic-imported with the card — nothing is pulled) and both it and the Pricing overview summarize them with [alignmentSummary.js](src/features/pricing/lib/alignmentSummary.js); its rows open Pricing on that channel (router state `{ pricingTab: 'alignment', alignSite }`).

### Accessibility conventions (pass of 2026-10-01 — keep them)

An axe-core scan of every route, in both themes, went from 1,107 violations to 0. Two small look changes were OK'd by the user:
- Small Wix text on its 15% tint uses `text-brand-wix-ink` (#0a5fd9 in light mode, 4.7:1).
- Links inside a sentence are underlined (`underline underline-offset-2`).

**Modals and file inputs**
- Build modals on the shared [Dialog](src/components/ui/Dialog.jsx).
  - It keeps a stack, so only the top dialog answers Escape and traps Tab.
  - `onClose` is read through a ref, so a new function on every render doesn't re-run the focus logic.
  - The backdrop closes only when the press started on the backdrop.
  - `subtitle` (or `ariaDescribedby`) is read as the description; confirm messages are linked the same way.
- A file input inside a label is `sr-only`, never `hidden`, so Tab reaches it ([FileDropzone](src/components/ui/FileDropzone.jsx)).
- **A box that scrolls sideways is `relative` when it holds screen-reader text** (sweep of 2026-11-10): an `sr-only` element is absolutely placed, so its containing block was `<main>`, and from inside a wide table it made `<main>` itself scroll sideways on a phone (product tab strip, Media's documents, Where to Buy table, Price Alignment table). A table that scrolls sideways takes `tabIndex={0}` (axe scrollable-region-focusable). Buttons revealed on hover also show where there is no hover (`[@media(hover:none)]:opacity-100`). **Small controls in dense tables get a 24 px touch area without changing the look** (user, 2026-11-10: "que se vea bien"): invisible padding cancelled by a negative margin — the shared [Checkbox](src/components/ui/Checkbox.jsx) label is `p-1 -m-1`, a raw checkbox goes inside `<label className="inline-flex p-1 -m-1">`, a SKU link in a row is `inline-block py-[3px] -my-[3px]`, a sort header button `py-1 -my-1`, an icon button `p-[5px]`. Media's language chips (21 px) were left as they are. Walmart's small text on its tint uses `text-brand-walmart-ink`.

**Rows and toggles**
- Rows that open or expand use a real link or a `<button>` in a cell. Never put `role="button"` or `aria-expanded` on a `<tr>`.
- Collapsed panels get `inert`.
- Toggles carry `aria-pressed` or `aria-expanded`.
- [FilterDropdown](src/features/products/components/FilterDropdown.jsx) moves focus into its portaled menu.
- Media images reorder with **Shift + ← / →**, the keyboard twin of drag-and-drop ([MediaSection](src/features/media/components/MediaSection.jsx) `moveImage`).
  - It uses the same reorder math and the same `persistReorder` save as a drop.
  - It moves one step and never goes in front of the pinned Primary / SinksDirect main.
  - Alt + ← is avoided because it is the browser's Back shortcut.

**Login**
- When the Turnstile check can't load, the form stops waiting for its token and says so; the server still enforces the captcha.

**Names and announcements**
- Icon-only buttons need `aria-label`; lucide icons are `aria-hidden`.
- Results and errors take `role="status"` / `role="alert"` on the element that is conditionally rendered.
- Two Pagination bars on one page need distinct `label`s.
- The top search remembers the products opened from it (user idea 2026-10-05, [recentSearches.js](src/features/search/lib/recentSearches.js)). It keeps the last 4 per user in localStorage and shows them as "Recent" when the empty bar is clicked.
  - The panel is the same combobox / listbox as the results; Clear empties the list.
  - The Delete key removes the highlighted product. The × on each row is its mouse twin and is `aria-hidden`, because a listbox holds only options.
  - **Its rows are real links** (user, 2026-11-20: "no puedo darles click derecho y abrir en otra pestaña"): each result and each Recent row is a `<Link role="option">` to the product's page, and "View all results" a link to the catalog's search, so the browser's own menu, Ctrl / Cmd + click and the middle button open them in another tab ([Topbar](src/components/layout/Topbar.jsx): `clickRow` leaves such a click to the browser — `forElsewhere` — and this tab's panel stays open with what was typed). **Ctrl / Cmd + Enter** is the keyboard twin: the highlighted row in a new tab, or the catalog's search when no row is highlighted. A plain click, the arrows and Enter are as before. A product opened in another tab from the results is remembered in Recent like any other. A row that opens a page is a link, never a `<button>` with `navigate()`.

**Shell and verification**
- AppShell has a "Skip to main content" link to `main#main-content`.
- `document.title` follows the route via `PAGE_TITLES` in [App.jsx](src/App.jsx); add new routes there.
- Verify with axe (`axe.min.js` injected through Playwright) and a pixel diff against HEAD.

### Layout conventions (pass of 2026-10-02 — keep them)

User decision: every page sits at the same place, so nothing jumps when moving between sections.
- **One left edge.** Every page renders straight in AppShell's container (`max-w-[1400px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-8`). A list page is full width; a narrow page keeps its `max-w-*` but is LEFT-aligned (never `mx-auto`) — Pricing, Settings, Users, Activity, Import, the channel pages. The product page is the one centred page (`max-w-6xl mx-auto`): its section rail needs the left gutter.
- **One page title.** `<h1 className="text-display-lg text-on-surface">` with its description `text-body-md text-on-surface-variant mt-1` under it and the page actions on the right (`flex items-end justify-between gap-4 flex-wrap`). No icon tile next to the title; a channel page keeps its channel logo.
- **`<main>` is `relative` with `[scrollbar-gutter:stable]`.** `relative` keeps absolutely placed children (sr-only labels, live regions) inside its scroll — without it they made the whole window scroll on Dashboard and Settings. The stable gutter keeps a page at the same place whether it scrolls or not.
- **Product section rail.** It docks 24 px left of the cards on every tab: an `absolute right-full` box on the page column with a `sticky` nav, never placed from the window width (it was `50vw − 640px`, and the scrollbar moved the cards but not the rail). It shows from 1880 px, where the rail and its gap clear the sidebar; below that the tab strip stays pinned.
- **The top bar's menus are drawn over the page** (user, 2026-11-11: the account panel was cut by the EOS ScoreCard's pinned toolbar). The Topbar is `z-[35]`: a bar a page pins inside `<main>` stays at `z-30` or lower (it comes later in the page, so at the same level it painted over the top bar's panels), the phone menu's backdrop is `z-40`, dialogs `z-50`. **A menu closes on an outside press through a `pointerdown` listener, never a see-through `fixed inset-0` layer**: the layer took the mouse wheel, so the page could not be scrolled while the account panel was open ([AccountMenu](src/components/layout/AccountMenu.jsx)). The small menus of Catalog (Full Report), Import and the bulk bar still use such a layer.
- **Phones (390 px).** No sideways scroll: tab groups scroll inside themselves (`max-w-full overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide`), button rows wrap, and a full-bleed strip uses the page's own padding (`-mx-4 sm:-mx-6`).
- Verify with real scrollbars (Playwright `ignore_default_args=['--hide-scrollbars']`) at 1920, 1440 and 390: the same title x on every page, and the document never scrolls (`documentElement.scrollHeight === clientHeight`).
- **A crash never blanks the window** (2026-10-05). [ErrorBoundary](src/components/ui/ErrorBoundary.jsx) wraps every page (ProtectedLayout, `resetKey` = the route, so it clears on navigation) and the whole app (main.jsx, `fullScreen`).
  - The fallback: "Something went wrong on this page", Reload page / Go to Dashboard and the technical details. A failed chunk of an old deploy reads "A new version of the PIM is available".
  - A tab opened before a deploy reloads itself when a file of the old build is gone (`vite:preloadError` in main.jsx, at most once every 30 s per page); otherwise the import fails with its own error — never `event.preventDefault()` without reloading, or the import resolves to undefined ("Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'fillWayfairPriceChangeFile')", 2026-10-16). Pricing's `lazyLib` turns an empty module into "A new version of the PIM is available — reload the page".
  - Never call a TipTap editor without checking `!editor.isDestroyed`: `useEditor` destroys and rebuilds its instance when effects are torn down, and `getHTML()` on the old one blanked the page on Edit.

### One look across the app (pass of 2026-11-11 — keep it)

User, 2026-11-11: "dale un repaso a toda la app", after "revisa todo para que todo esté parejo y todo acorde al diseño global". Every page was opened with live data at 1440 and 390 in both themes; what was uneven took the variant most of the app already used. There is no shared Button or Card component: the classes are written where they are used, so copy these.
- **One button.** A pill, `text-label-md` (12 px), on pages, cards and dialogs alike:
  - primary `inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-primary text-on-primary text-label-md font-semibold hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity disabled:opacity-50`;
  - outlined `inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-full border border-outline-variant text-label-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-colors` (no weight class: the label is already medium; no fill of its own; never `border-outline`);
  - inside a dense row or a toolbar the same button is `px-3 py-1.5`.
  - Until that day the product page's header, Listing Health, Analytics, Import, the ScoreCard and seven dialogs (the app's confirm among them) drew theirs at 16 px in the light weight; Pricing at 14 px; and 45 buttons had a squarer corner (`rounded-lg`: the Where to Buy panels, Settings, the promotion card's actions, the Wix card, bulk edit). Left as they are: the Sidebar's New Product (it takes the menu items' shape), the catalog's bulk bar and the small text buttons inside rows.
- **One card.** `rounded-2xl border border-outline-variant bg-surface-container-lowest`. Never `bg-surface` for a card: in the light theme the two are the same white, in the dark theme `surface` is lighter than the page and `lowest` darker, so Pricing, Settings, Users and the Activity Log read differently from the rest there. A promotion's card and Pricing's empty states had no border at rest. `bg-surface` stays for what floats (dialogs, menus) and for fields.
- **`cn()` knows the type scale** ([cn.js](src/lib/cn.js), `TYPE_SCALE`). tailwind-merge took `text-label-md` for a colour: beside `text-on-surface` it kept whichever came last and dropped the other, without a word — the ScoreCard's tabs and market switch had lost their size, its "Measurable" / "Target" header cells were bigger than the week headers, and the avatar had lost its colour. The ScoreCard's classes now say the sizes it was approved at (tabs `text-body-md`, market switch `text-body-sm`), so only those two header cells changed there. A new `--text-*` token goes on that list.
- **The type scale has no `text-title-sm`** (five headings used it and fell back to whatever they inherited): the titles are `text-title-lg` and `text-title-md`.
- **Two buttons never share a name.** Listing Health → Sinks Direct Canada had two "Refresh from Wix": the store's catalog in one pull keeps the name, the one that reads every linked product one by one is "Read each product"; they sit together on the right.
- Found on the way and fixed: the score ranges under Listing Health's tiles were at 60% opacity (3.2:1 in the light theme); the product page's "Master Carton" heading skipped a level; the Aliases table's last header was empty; Listing Health's product table made `<main>` scroll sideways on a phone (its scroll box was not `relative`, see Accessibility).
- **Checked with** `impeccable detect` (0 findings on the changed files) and the tour of every page (36 screens × 1440 / 390 × both themes): axe's only finding is the top bar's account button, no sideways scroll, the document never scrolls. Not opened by the tour: the Users table and the store cards of a product (they read through edge functions, which the tour does not call) and dialogs — their buttons were evened in the code.
- **Impeccable** (impeccable.style) is installed as a project skill since that day (`.claude/skills/impeccable`, `.claude/agents/impeccable-*`; `npx impeccable install --providers=claude --scope=project --no-hooks`). Its engine (`scripts/bin/`, 21 MB) is not in git: the launcher downloads it again when it is missing. Its hook, telemetry and live mode are the user's to switch. The project has no PRODUCT.md / DESIGN.md yet (`/impeccable init`, `/impeccable document`).

### Progress cards (2026-10-31 — keep them)

User request: every slow thing shows its percentage on screen — downloads / exports, imports / uploads and pushes to channels ("que salga en pantalla cuál es el porcentaje"; the user chose a floating card, bottom right, like a browser's downloads). **Never for a page loading its data** (user, the same day, after trying it on Listing Health / Dashboard: "no me gusta que se muestre cuando cargan las páginas … solo al exportar cosas o subir cosas"): pages keep their own skeletons and spinners.
- **The store** is [taskProgress.js](src/lib/taskProgress.js), a plain module so `api/`, `exports/` and `pricing/lib/` code reports from where it loops: `const task = startTask('Wayfair export', { detail })` → `task.progress(share)` / `task.count(done, total, detail)` / `task.part(from, to)` (a slice of the bar, nestable) / `task.counter()` (a `(done, total)` callback) → `task.finish(detail)`, `task.fail(err)` (red, stays until closed) or `task.cancel()`; `withTask(title, fn)`. A card shows only once a task has run 400 ms (fast runs never flash); the bar never moves back; no reading = an indeterminate stripe (a single edge-function call, a Storage upload / download — they report nothing).
- **The cards** are [TaskProgressDock](src/components/ui/TaskProgressDock.jsx), mounted in AppShell, so they stay while the person changes page. PromoTaskNudge sits above them (`--task-dock-h`). A bar fixed to the bottom with buttons — the catalog's bulk actions, the Wix card's sticky push footer — registers through [useDockClearance](src/components/ui/useDockClearance.js) (`--dock-clear-h`) so the cards sit above it instead of covering its buttons; a new bottom bar does the same. Screen readers hear a task start, end or fail, never each percent. Dev console: `demoTask()` / `demoTask(true)`.
- **The pattern:** the UI handler starts the card and passes `task` (or `task.part(a, b)`) as an OPTIONAL last option `{ task = null } = {}`; inside, every call is `task?.…`, so other callers behave as before. A progress change never changes what an operation does or produces. Every path ends the card (early returns, a dialog the person backs out of → `cancel()`, unmount → `cancel()`); one card per user action (a multi-template export is one card with stages).
- Shared helpers already report: `buildSectionedWorkbook(sheets, { task })` (sheets, then compression), templateFiller's `openTemplate(path, { task })`, `downloadZip(zip, name, ext, { task })`, `downloadMediaZip(entries, name, { task })` (files fetched, then parts) and `labelTask(task, 'File 2 of 3')`; Pricing's `batchTicker` / `blockingStep` ([fileProgress.js](src/features/pricing/lib/fileProgress.js)).
- Synchronous work blocks repaints: `await task.step('Building the sheets…')` (or `await task.paint()`) BEFORE a blocking stage, never per item of a big loop (each waits for a frame). In a hidden tab they return at once, so a run left in another tab never stalls.
- A new slow operation gets a card the same way.

**The info sections of a Wix product page are always in this order** (user, 2026-11-04: "estrictamente Dimensions, Features, Technical Documents, Where to Buy" — WHERE TO BUY last, never first): `orderWixSections` in [_shared/wixSectionOrder.js](supabase/functions/_shared/wixSectionOrder.js), applied by the product push (`deriveWixSectionsFromPim`) and the WHERE TO BUY publish (`withWhereToBuySection`, which also writes a page whose only change is the order). Any other section (Recommended Accessories, Videos) keeps its place after the documents and before WHERE TO BUY. Pages get it when they are next written (the 23 Azuni pages on 2026-11-04).

**Each Wix store card scores Listing Health with its own definition** (user, 2026-11-04: "aquí también se debe medir el where to buy"): `ProductHealthBadge marketplace={cfg.channel}`. Sinks Direct Canada keeps `wix` (the product's columns); every other store is scored from the card's live read the way the daily snapshot scores it (`_wixSite`: that store's own link — no longer the Sinks Direct Canada id —, name, price against the expected price, description, images, section titles). The Stylish and Azuni pages also have **Where to Buy Tab** (`ws_section_where_to_buy`), measured only while the PIM has links to show there (`expectedWhereToBuyHtml` in [whereToBuy.js](src/features/products/api/whereToBuy.js), the publish's own rule; "si no hay link, no hay where to buy"). A field with `applies` is left out of the score where it does not apply; the daily snapshot sets no `_wtbExpected`, so it does not measure it.

### "Push <store>" on the product page (2026-11-04)

User request: every marketplace card of the product's Marketplaces tab has ONE "Push <store>" button that sends everything the PIM has, "tal cual como PIM lo recomienda", after a warning whose "I accept that it goes up as the PIM recommends it" box must be ticked ([ChannelPushBar](src/features/syndication/components/ChannelPushBar.jsx), shown above the chosen card to editors; not for an excluded store or a brand the store does not sell). The old "Push SinksDirect" / "Push Stylish" buttons over the tiles are gone (user's choice). What each store takes:
- **Wix stores** (Sinks Direct, Stylish, Azuni): `pushProductToWixSite(sku, site)` in wixSync.js — the content (description auto-formatted, prices, weight; never the name), the info sections rebuilt from the PIM (dimensions, features, the documents section) and the whole gallery, the same steps as the brand-wide `pushProductToAllWixSites` (`pushEverythingToSite`); on a Stylish store or the Azuni store also the WHERE TO BUY links (`publishWhereToBuy`, the audit's `sync`). A product not on that store yet is created first, visible and out of stock.
  - **A new product goes up VISIBLE and OUT OF STOCK on every Wix store** (user, 2026-11-06: "siempre debe ir visible pero en out of stock … Odoo se encargará de darle inventario"), whatever `visible_online` says; `wix-create-product` is the one place. Wix's own stock default is not relied on: created hidden → `PATCH /stores/v2/inventoryItems/product/{id}` (`trackQuantity: true`, `quantity: 0`, the way all five stores keep their products: a sample of 60 on that day) → read back → shown. It is never visible and in stock: if it can't be set to 0 it is deleted again and nothing is linked, so a retry starts clean. A product Wix already has is linked as it is, stock untouched. Until that day a create said "hidden" and the push right after it made it visible anyway (`visible_online` is true on 377 of 378). `wix-read-product` also returns `stock` and `visible`. Checked that day: the inventory call answers 200 on all five stores, writing the same 0 on a product already at 0 (nothing changed). No test product was created, since Odoo's connector could import it, so the first real create is the first full run.
- **Wayfair CA / USA**: `pushEverythingToWayfair` in wayfairSync.js — the spec attributes, then images, videos and documents, without the review dialog (a spec refusal does not stop the media). Title, description, bullets and prices never travel. Not linked → disabled (Canada: list it first; USA: Wayfair copies new listings from Canada).
- **Best Buy sells at MAP CAD** (Listing Health's `bb_price_matches`, the snapshot's `with_diffs` in bestbuySync.js and health-refresh: the offer's own `origin_price` — Mirakl's `price` is the discounted one while a promotion runs — within the Price Alignment margin; until 2026-11-10 it was compared with the MSRP and almost every offer failed: score 86 → 94). **Best Buy Canada** (user, 2026-11-06: "agrega push best buy y mappea todos los campos necesarios"; a Best Buy tile and [BestBuyProductCard](src/features/syndication/components/BestBuyProductCard.jsx), which shows every field a push sends): `pushProductToBestBuy` in bestbuySync.js, one row of Mirakl's P41 content import built by [bestbuyContent.js](src/features/syndication/lib/bestbuyContent.js), also used by the channel page's Push Content (which took only title, descriptions and 5 images, no accessories, and sent short descriptions of 500 characters where Best Buy says 400). Mapped from Best Buy's own PM11 / VL11 for the four categories our 281 offers on a PIM SKU sit in: Other Kitchen & Dining (CAT_660943: sinks, kitchen faucets), Bathroom Sink Faucets (CAT_37129: bathroom faucets AND 34 bathroom sinks, as Best Buy has them; the faucet-only fields skip the sinks), Kitchen & Dining Accessories (CAT_314937), Bathroom Accessories (CAT_328963). The offer decides the category and the UPC (hands off: 6 combos' UPC on Best Buy differs from the PIM's — K131GS01G, K131NS01N, K-107SR, K131NK142N, K131SK142S, K131SS01S). Images: up to 10 of the Canada set, the white main first; the gray hero (`image_role` sinksdirect_main, Wix's main) never goes (user, the same day: "solo se envía la foto fondo blanco, la de Wix no"), and a product with no white main can't go. Required: title, short description (≤ 400, whole sentences), brand (as Best Buy writes it), UPC, model and part number = SKU, main image, Brand New; on kitchen accessories also the type (from the name) and Dishwasher Safe: Yes only where the PIM's text says so, never on bamboo (the text is about the set's container: A-907, A-913), else No (A-922, S-01G, A-900RD, DA-04, ST-08, H-24). Best Buy's axes: width = the PIM's length, depth = its width, height = its depth (faucets only height); cm, inches, kg, L/min, number only. A field the PIM has no value for is LEFT OUT, never an empty cell, and rows travel by their set of columns (22 sets), since an empty cell could wipe Best Buy's value. No French fields exist but the warranty URL. The same for every product (user, 2026-11-06): French Compliant Y, Energy Star Indicator N, Trim / Accent Material None (only Other Kitchen & Dining has it). Left out on purpose: the French warranty URL (`/fr/warranty` is still in English), Variant Group Code (it would merge listings), Sales Tax Code, Web Hierarchy. Checked 2026-11-06 against the live attribute and value lists: 281 of 281 rows, no error. The user's first real import (2026-11-06, C234L as a new product, import 1954283) was accepted: 1 line read, 1 in success, 0 errors, status SENT, waiting for Best Buy's review. `GET /products/imports` lists nothing on this account, so read an import by its id, `GET /products/imports/{id}` (the id is in the Activity Log and `bestbuy_new_listings`), and `/transformed_file` shows what Best Buy took.
  - **A product not on Best Buy yet** (user, the same day: "necesitamos crear productos desde 0 y actualizarlos también"; "yo elijo qué oferta crear", "no crees nada tú, lo haré yo"; "¿se le puede agregar el offer de una vez?"): **a person chooses every product: create / offer / want_offer refuse the service-role key and the cron, and the only thing that goes by itself is the offer asked for with a product.** The edge function `bestbuy-new-listing` (table `bestbuy_new_listings`, [20261106_bestbuy_new_listings.sql](supabase/migrations/20261106_bestbuy_new_listings.sql)): `create` sends the product through the content import, in the category chosen on its card (`defaultBestBuyCategory`: where its kind already is on Best Buy; drains and plates Bathroom Accessories, other accessories Kitchen & Dining), for Best Buy's review. Best Buy takes no offer until the product exists (P31 `product_references=UPC-A|<upc>`, the only reference type it answers to), so the card's box, on by default (`offer_wanted`, `want_offer` while in review), asks for it, and `check` sends it at the first look after approval: cron `bestbuy-new-listing-hourly` (:50, user's OK the same day; `verify_jwt` off, x-cron-secret, check only), or an editor opening that product's card or the Best Buy list. With the box off the status goes to `approved` and the button waits. `offer` is that button: for a product Best Buy already has. **The offer fills only two things** (user: "el que dice Price sería el MAP Blue; Warranty parts and labor es siempre 0; solo se llenan esas dos cosas"; [_shared/bestbuyOffer.js](supabase/functions/_shared/bestbuyOffer.js)): price = MAP Blue CAD, read when it goes, and `manufacturer-warranty` = 0, plus who it is (SKU, UPC, state 11). No stock, shipping class or lead time: it starts at 0 units and Stock sync gave it its stock (the PIM's Best Buy stock sync is off since 2026-11-10: Odoo sends the stock now) (our 281 older offers have 2 days and SMALL / MED; read the first new offer to see what Best Buy assigned). `lookup` / `candidates` only read. **22 of the 97 products with no offer were already in the catalog on 2026-11-06, so they need only the offer. A listing that plainly is another kind of product is refused (`catalogMismatch`): the UPCs of D-701N2, D-702B2, D-702C2 and D-702N2 (drains) are faucet combos on Best Buy, and an offer there would sell a drain on a faucet's page; UPCs are hands off, report it.** Syndication → Best Buy → "Products not on Best Buy" lists them to choose from, and opens the product. Best Buy answers 429 to a few `/offers` reads in a row: the function waits and retries, and the list takes the offers from the latest snapshot instead of paging them. A live offer's warranty does not follow any rule (5 Years reads 365, 1000, 10095, 100095): not touched.
- **Walmart USA**: only while it is not listed — an offer by match (`submitWalmartItems`, production). Listed → disabled until the brand is approved on Walmart's Brand Portal.
- **Walmart Canada**: disabled — its API takes no product content.
Each push runs a progress card, writes the Activity Log ("accepted as the PIM recommends") and re-reads the store's card.

**The catalog's bulk "Push to Wix" picks the stores** (user, 2026-11-05: "¿hace push a Azuni también?" — it did not: it sent only the content, only to Sinks Direct Canada, for the products with the legacy `wix_product_id`). [WixStoresPushDialog](src/features/syndication/components/WixStoresPushDialog.jsx) lists the five stores with how many of the selection each would get (brand rule and exclusions applied; a store none of them is sold on is disabled), checked where there is something to push, and each product gets the same full push as its page's "Push <store>" (`pushProductToWixSite(sku, site, { create: false })`: content, sections rebuilt, gallery, and the Where to Buy on Stylish / Azuni), two at a time, one progress card. A bulk push never creates: a product not on a store yet is listed as skipped (create it from its page). "Refresh from Wix" still reads Sinks Direct Canada only (its `wix_raw` cache).

### EOS ScoreCard (2026-11-03)

User request: the company's weekly EOS Scorecard ("2026 Company ScoreCard.xlsx") as a PIM page, `/scorecard` ("EOS ScoreCard" in the sidebar, every role). Only the marketplace sheets (user: "Lowes Score Card, Wayfair Scorecard y así"): one tab per sheet — Amazon, Wayfair, Home Depot (Canada, and USA since 2026-11-09 — user; board key still `homedepot_ca`, no weekly sheet in the workbook, same rows as Canada), Lowe's, Rona, BB&B / Overstock, Sinks Direct ([boards.js](src/features/scorecard/lib/boards.js)), plus Menards, Best Buy (Canada) and Walmart (Canada / USA) (user, 2026-11-09; no weekly sheet in the workbook: every row is the PIM's, Odoo marketplaces 12, 11 and 3, and what each lists = the products with its Where to Buy link); every tab shares one set of names and one order (`position`, user 2026-11-09; before-state in that day's Activity Log); the quarterly report sheets ("Home Depot ", "Lowe's", Rona, Menards) and the company / ops sheets are not in it.
- **Tables** ([20261103_eos_scorecard.sql](supabase/migrations/20261103_eos_scorecard.sql)): `scorecard_metrics` (board, market, name, source, owner_id or owner_name, unit number / percent / money / hours / days / text, goal + goal_op — the row's Target, see below —, auto_key, position, archived) and `scorecard_values` (metric_id, week_start = the week's SUNDAY, value, note = what was typed when it is not only a number, origin manual / auto / import). Percentages are stored as fractions and typed as percent numbers (95 = 95 %).
- **Weeks run Sunday to Saturday** (user's choice); W1 = the year's first Sunday (2026: Jan 4), the workbook's own numbering — its W44 is the week of Sunday Nov 1, the column the team fills that week ([weeks.js](src/features/scorecard/lib/weeks.js)). **Layout (user's choice of 2026-11-04, "se ve raro" about the first long table):** the EOS grid, one market at a time (Canada / USA toggle, `?board=&market=`), rows grouped by theme ([sections.js](src/features/scorecard/lib/sections.js): Pricing & promotions, Sales, Sales Ads, Catalog / stock / reviews, Shipping & service, Returns & refunds, Actions & notes; Sales and Sales Ads were one theme, "Sales & ads", until 2026-11-16 (user: "separa lo de Sales & Ads en Sales y aparte Sales Ads") — the ads rows are told by their name (ads, CPC, keyword, ACoS, spent), so "Ads Sales" and "Total Spent with 0 Sales" are ads; a tab with no ads row shows no Sales Ads — `scorecard_metrics.section`, NULL = read from the name, [20261104_scorecard_sections.sql](supabase/migrations/20261104_scorecard_sections.sql); the pencil's "Theme" sets it), one line per row (owner initials, the name truncated with owner / source in its tooltip, a PIM chip, the Target column), and a strip for the latest week: filled, on target, off target, without a target, "of yours to fill". The table fits AppShell's full container (1,336 px) with no sideways scroll — a week column shows "W44 / Nov 1", a note longer than 7 characters is an icon (text in the tooltip and sr-only) — so keep new content from widening the week columns. Narrower, it scrolls inside itself and opens on the latest week. A dot marks a value the PIM filled. Hovering a week's header shows at once the days its numbers cover ("W45 · numbers of Sun, Nov 1 – Sat, Nov 7, 2026", "In progress — numbers so far, updated every hour" / "Upcoming"; user, 2026-11-06) — a fixed tooltip drawn in `<body>`, since the header boxes clip anything below them. Typing a value never resizes the grid (user, 2026-11-05): the cell's input takes the cell's own box (`p-0` cell, `h-[2.25rem]` like the button, no minimum width, `size={1}` so an input's default 20-character width does not count in the column sizing). Checked 2026-11-04 with a stand-in harness (real data, fake auth): axe 0 at 1920 / 1440 / 390 in both themes, the document never scrolls. **Pinned while scrolling** (user, 2026-11-05: "fijar los marketplaces … para poder cambiar rápido"): from lg the controls — marketplace tabs, weeks, Canada / USA, the week's summary, Refresh — are `lg:sticky` at the top of `<main>` (a line under them once pinned; on a phone they wrap to a third of the screen, so they scroll there) and their height goes to `--sc-toolbar-h`; the table's week header pins right under them. When the 13 weeks fit (≥ ~1,650 px wide) the table box is `overflow-clip` (no scroll box, so its own `<thead>` is sticky); when they do not (1440 needs ~1,231–1,311 px in a 1,103 px box; phones) the box scrolls sideways, which stops a sticky header, so an aria-hidden copy of the header row (the real columns' measured widths, `table-layout: fixed`, the box's scrollLeft) is pinned over it — the real header stays for screen readers. Checked the same day (live data, writes and functions blocked): pinned at 1920 / 1440 / 390, columns aligned, axe 0 in both themes, the document never scrolls.
- **Who writes** (user's choice, enforced by RLS `scorecard_can_write` / `scorecard_can_replace`, [20261104_scorecard_pim_rows_locked.sql](supabase/migrations/20261104_scorecard_pim_rows_locked.sql)): everyone reads; a measurable's OWNER types its numbers; admins edit every other number and the rows (Add measurable / the pencil / Remove = `archived`, history kept). An owner without a PIM user (Abhijit) is `owner_name`: admins type those. Every typed value and row change writes the Activity Log.
- **The PIM's numbers can't be edited** (user, 2026-11-04: "los valores de PIM no pueden editarse"): a row with an `auto_key` is read-only for everyone, admins included — its cells are no buttons, and RLS takes only origin `auto` there (from any editor's session) and no delete. Until 2026-11-23 an admin could turn "Filled by the PIM" off in the pencil and type the row by hand. **Since 2026-11-23 what the PIM fills is locked for everyone, whole** (user: "veo que hay cosas que llena PIM, entonces eso no puede ser editable por NADIE, si PIM lo llena debe quedar bloqueado"; [20261123_scorecard_pim_locked.sql](supabase/migrations/20261123_scorecard_pim_locked.sql)):
  - **A PIM row has no pencil**: nobody renames it, removes it, gives it an owner or hands it back to typing from the app, and the trigger `scorecard_metrics_pim_locked` refuses it to any person's session ("This measurable is filled by the PIM: only its target can be changed."). Its **Target** stays the admins' to set (from its cell) and its position can move; a typed row can still be made a PIM row with the pencil (the dialog says it is then locked). Handing a PIM row back to typing is done in the database (the service role and the crons carry no user and pass).
  - **A number the PIM wrote is locked on any row**, also on one people type today — the 48 rows that left ShipStation on 2026-11-17 keep the PIM's W44–W46: their cells with the PIM's dot are no buttons, and `scorecard_can_replace(metric, origin)` refuses to replace or clear a value of origin `auto` on a typed row (the app's save answers with the database's refusal; a plain update changes 0 rows). The weeks after them are typed as before.
  - Checked that day: 20 cases as an admin, an editor and the service role, inside an undone transaction before applying and again on the live database after — a PIM row renamed, unlocked, removed, given an owner or deleted: refused; its target and position: taken; a typed row renamed or made a PIM row: taken; a PIM-written number on a typed row typed over, changed or cleared: refused or 0 rows; a new week and a typed number on that row: taken; the browser's own fill of a PIM row (origin auto): taken; the service role: as before. The app's change (no pencil, no button) was linted and built, not opened in a browser.
- **Mixed rows** (user's choice): a row with an `auto_key` ([autoMetrics.js](src/features/scorecard/lib/autoMetrics.js)) gets THIS week's number from the PIM through the `scorecard-fill` crons (user, 2026-11-09: "para que los datos se recarguen toca entrar en la tab, no me gusta") AND when an editor opens its tab, if the number is older than 15 minutes (user, 2026-11-10: "sí es mejor que se carguen cuando se abra la tab"; `fillAuto`, `computeAutoValues` on the same shared code), or with "Refresh PIM numbers"; saved with origin `auto` — it REPLACES a number typed or imported this week (Amazon USA's W44 came from the workbook); past weeks keep what they hold. Keys: `map_pct` (Price Alignment of the board's targets, aligned ÷ comparable — the same reports as the Pricing tab), `promo_skus` (the monthly promotion running today, the market's list minus products switched off on every one of the board's channels), `flash_skus` / `event_skus` / `flash_count` / `event_count` / `deal_count` (flash deals / special events whose `marketplaces` hold the board's channels and whose window overlaps the week). They differ from what the team typed before (e.g. the PIM's promo list counts every member, not only the products listed on that store): until 2026-11-23 an admin could turn a row's "Filled by the PIM" off in its dialog; since then a PIM row is locked for everyone (the bullet above).
- **Server-filled rows — Sinks Direct from Odoo** (user, 2026-11-04 / 11-05): the edge function `scorecard-fill` (secrets `ODOO_URL` / `ODOO_DB` / `ODOO_USER` / `ODOO_API_KEY`, the user's own read-only key; boards with `serverFill` in boards.js, `BOARDS` in the function — Sinks Direct Canada / USA = Odoo `sale.order` with `wix_site_name` sinksdirect.ca / sinksdirectusa.com). A column (its Sunday) holds the week BEFORE it, Eastern days. Keys: `sales` (untaxed total of the non-cancelled orders, shipping charged included — Odoo's own sales report), `orders` (orders with at least one product sold with a price: a $0 order — spare part, warranty replacement, open box sent free, 141 of 445 since August — is no order, nor is one that only charges shipping), `units` (quantity of the product lines with a price, not the "Shipping Charges" service; grids and parts sold count), `skus_not_sold_90d`, `oos_loss` (products at 0 units now × their average weekly sales of the 8 weeks before), `obsolete_skus`, `listings_updated` (products the PIM pushed to that store, Activity Log). Second round the same day (user: "que la mayoría de campos se llenen automáticamente"), from **the store's own Wix orders and reviews** (`WIX_API_KEY`) and Odoo's deliveries / returns / costs — Odoo records almost no cancellation (1 Sinks Direct order in 2026), no refund (0 credit notes) and no ticket (Helpdesk empty): `cancel_rate` (the store's paid orders of the week now CANCELED), `on_time_ship` / `late_ship_rate` (**until 2026-11-17 — typed rows since, see "ShipStation is out of the ScoreCard" below**: the week's paid Odoo orders fully shipped by the end of the **2nd business day** after the order, Mon–Fri Eastern; a delivery ships when its **ShipStation label** was made — never Odoo's done date: on 2026-10-16 a batch of June–August Sinks Direct deliveries was validated in Odoo that day; late = after it, or not shipped with the deadline gone; still in time = left out; a delivery done in Odoo with no label we know — a pickup, a label made outside ShipStation — leaves its order out, never guessed), `odr` (the week's paid store orders cancelled, refunded or returned), `returns_not_received` ($ refunded in the week — read from the order's payments — on orders Odoo SHIPPED and got no return back for: a refund before shipping returns nothing; both "$ of retunrs not received" and "Return no received $" rows, unit money), `reviews_5plus` (PIM products with more than 5 approved Wix reviews on the store — Sinks Direct USA's ~11,900 were loaded by an import app, origin APP; Canada's 74 are customers'), `no_profit_skus` (products whose store price this week — the running monthly promotion, else MAP — is under Odoo's average cost of that market's company — Stylish International CAD = company 2, Stylish USA USD = 1 — plus — until 2026-11-17: the cost alone since, ShipStation being out of the ScoreCard — the SKU's **real net shipping cost per unit** of the last 180 days: each delivery's ShipStation label cost, in the market's currency, shared among the products in THAT box by value (an accessory sent alone carries its box; a kit is its own line), minus the shipping the customer paid (the order's "Shipping Charges" line, shared over the order by value — small accessory orders pay ~$19, which is why ST-01 / ST-03 are not flagged); an order with an unknown, $0 or other-currency label is left out whole; a SKU not shipped in that time is judged on its cost alone. 2026-11-05: Canada A-909 ($30 < 30.16 + 8.60), USA none). Odoo carries no shipping cost at all (`shipstation_shipping_cost` and `carrier_price` are 0 everywhere); every Sinks Direct label checked on 2026-11-05 had one (Purolator, Canpar, UPS — the $0 labels were other channels' FedEx).
- **Every marketplace tab is server-filled too** (user, 2026-11-05: "revisa qué más podemos automatizar"; `serverFill` on every board): Odoo holds every marketplace's orders (`sale.order.marketplace_name` = marketplace.master — Wayfair 4, Home Depot 5, Rona 6, Lowe's 7, BB&B 8, Amazon FBM 10, Amazon FBA 13, Overstock 17 — and `company_id` 2 = Canada, 1 = USA; ~99 % of their deliveries match a ShipStation label). `BOARDS` in scorecard-fill: `odoo` (the orders' domain; `via()` reads it from lines `order_id.` and pickings `sale_id.`), `company`, `stock` (our warehouse of the country; Amazon also `fba`, see the end of this bullet), `listed` (the SKUs the channel lists, for "# SKU not sold" and obsoletes: `product_aliases` Home Depot CA / Lowe's US / Rona / BB&B / Overstock, `amazon_links` ca, `wayfair_item_group_id` / `wayfair_usa_item_group_id`; Sinks Direct `catalog` = every product; Amazon.com `{ wtb: "amazon_us" }` since 2026-11-16, the products with an Amazon.com Where to Buy link), `ships` (the marketplaces WE ship: Amazon FBM only). User decisions: **Sales = what the marketplace pays us** (Odoo's untaxed total, in the company's currency — an order's own currency converted with its `currency_rate`; Wayfair Canada pays in USD), not the portal's retail sales (Home Depot CA Oct 18–24: Odoo $37,490, typed $33,737); **returns over 4 weeks** (`return_rate` = orders with a return Odoo received in the 4 weeks ending with the data week ÷ the paid orders placed in them; `returns_value` = the weekly average value of the goods back, returned qty × the order line's price — until 2026-11-24: the marketplace's credit memos of the week since, see the bullet after this one) because bodega validates returns in batches (a single week read 0 %, 58 %, 0 %); **on-time / late = the same 2-business-day label rule** as Sinks Direct (portals measure against each marketplace's own ship-by date) — until 2026-11-17: typed rows since. An order still open in Odoo 14 days after it was placed is left out of late (the marketplace cancelled it and Odoo was not told). Also `sales_usd_k` (Wayfair's "Total Sales. Thousands USD", USD orders as they are, others at Odoo's rate of today; unit set to number) and `azuni_skus_sold` (BB&B). Keys read from a Wix store itself (`store: true` in autoMetrics.js: cancel rate, ODR, returns not received, reviews, profitability) are offered only on Sinks Direct. 60 rows were switched on that day (audit rows); past typed values stay, empty past weeks got the PIM's. 135 of 289 rows are now PIM-filled. Still typed: Odoo has almost no marketplace cancellation and no refund, Amazon FBA (stock, returns, account health, buy box, inventory age), portal service metrics (CSAT, response time, unresponsive, replacement parts), ads (Amazon Ads, Wayfair, Google Ads), reviews on the stores (the Where to Buy page reads could add them), texts. **"Units Sold" is the PIM's on every marketplace tab** (user, 2026-11-16: "el KPI de units sold en todos los MKs … automático viniendo de Odoo"): Amazon USA's was the one still typed (in the workbook it sat among the ads rows) and took the key `units` that day — W44 19, W46 18; the 4 Jessica had typed for W45 an hour before stayed (Odoo: 23), since a typed past week is never replaced without `replaceTyped` — until he said "Reemplázalos" that afternoon (below). **The same day, at his word row by row, the last three marketplace rows the PIM could fill went to it, all on Amazon** (`scorecard-fill` v43; before / after on the Amazon tab: no other number changed): **Loss Due to Out of Stock** (Canada, USA; "también debe ser automatizado en todos los MKs") — Amazon sells from two stocks, what it holds itself (FBA: Odoo's warehouses AMZ-CAN / AMZ-USA, `fba: "AMZCAN"` / `"AMZUSA"`, kept by the transfers sent there, by every FBA order and by adjustments; `warehouseStock`, on hand less reserved, both companies allowed) and our warehouse (the FBM offers), and **every product Amazon holds none of counts, whatever we have** (user, the same day, asked which of the two rules: "Todo lo que esté en 0 en Amazon" — the first version counted a product only at 0 in both, 107 and 639 a week; v44): such a product loses what it sold from Amazon's own stock in the 8 weeks before (the Amazon FBA orders), and, with none in our warehouse either, what it sold from ours too (the orders we ship, `ships`); a product Amazon still holds loses nothing. W46: Canada 386 (S-131W, S-406T, S-825N, K-145N…), USA 1,240 (S-642WK, P-210H, S-200T, S-831WH…; the run's `diagnostics.outAtAmazon` lists them). Jessica had typed 1,371 and 868 for W45, from her own report. Amazon Mexico's orders sit in Odoo under the USA company as Amazon FBA, in pesos: the function turns them into USD by the order's own rate (a first estimate given to the user, 1,103 for the USA, had added them as dollars). **# SKUs Not Sold (90 Days)** on Amazon USA: what Amazon.com lists = the products with an Amazon.com Where to Buy link (339), as on Menards, Best Buy and Walmart — 240 in W46 (she had typed 161). **# SKUs with More Than 15 Reviews** (Canada, USA; new key `reviews_15plus`, this week's column only): the PIM products whose Amazon page shows more than 15 reviews — Amazon counts ratings — from `where_to_buy_links.review_count`, only a page that is this product's (the app's `linkReviews` rule); 27 and 27 that afternoon, with a third of the Amazon pages still to be read again since the reviews reader was deployed that morning, so it rises as they are (Price2Spy's 139 for the USA had not moved in six weeks). **Her typed W45 numbers were replaced where the PIM has a number for that week** (user, the same afternoon: "Reemplázalos"; `{ boards: ["amazon"], backfill: 1, refresh: 1, replaceTyped: true }` — one week back, so the workbook's W44 imports were not touched): Units Sold 4 → 23 and # SKUs Not Sold 161 → 236 on Amazon USA, both kept in that run's Activity Log entry. Loss Due to Out of Stock (1,371 Canada, 868 USA) and the reviews count (139) keep what she typed: the PIM reads those from today's stock and pages and has no number for a week that is over. **"Ads Units Sold" was added to the Amazon tab that day** (user: "vamos a agregar un unit sold para Sales Ads también … en Amazon"; Canada and USA, typed, under Sales Ads after Avg. CPC — the Sinks Direct tab already had the row). The 4 she had typed under Units Sold may have been that number: it is in the Activity Log entry of the replacement. **"# Listings Optimized per Week" is the PIM's on every tab that has it** (user, the same day: "debe ser de PIM también"; until then only Sinks Direct's was, and Amazon, Wayfair, Home Depot, Lowe's, Rona and BB&B / Overstock typed theirs from Asana — work done inside each portal, which the PIM does not see). Asked what the PIM should count, he chose **"lo que el PIM envía o exporta"**: per marketplace and week, the products the PIM pushed there (the Wix stores' pushes; Wayfair's API pushes of its supplier, never the sandbox or a refused one) or whose listing file for it — New listing or Update listing, never a price or promotion file — was downloaded from the PIM. The downloads were not noted anywhere, so each one now leaves one Activity Log entry with its products ([listingExportLog.js](src/features/syndication/lib/listingExportLog.js) `logListingExport(marketplace, purpose, skus)`: action `export`, entity `product`, target = the marketplace's key from `templateMarketplaceKey`, metadata `{ listing: true, purpose, skus }`), written where a file is generated: the product page's Export Templates and the catalog's Export Template (each exporter's branch, with the products written into the file — one an exporter leaves out is not in it); BB&B's exporter writes its own entry in that shape. **A new exporter's call site does the same.** `scorecard-fill` (v45; `exports` and `wayfair` on a board's source) counts those entries, each product once a week. **It counts from the week of 2026-11-16** (`LISTING_LOG_FROM`): a week that ended before it gets no number on those tabs instead of a 0 the PIM cannot vouch for — W46 keeps what was typed — and nothing is counted in production until the app with the logging is deployed. Checked that day: the function with made-up downloads injected in the test only (a product twice counts once, an unknown SKU is ignored, another marketplace's entry is not counted) and Sinks Direct's numbers as before; in a browser with a stand-in sign-in and every write captured, the product page (Wayfair CA, Home Depot US new listing, Amazon CA update: one entry each; a promotion file and an unknown store: none) and the catalog's bulk bar (Wayfair CA with two products without a template: only the one written; Lowe's set: its three products; Home Depot CA update without item numbers and Menards without a template: nothing noted). The Best Buy, Walmart and Menards tabs have no such row. A preview can try keys on rows that lack them: `{ mode: "preview", boards: [...], assume: [{ board, market, key }] }`. The cron sends ONE request per tab, all at once (`scorecard-fill-daily`: `body := b.body … from (values …) as b(body)` — every tab in one run passed 150 s once the AI notes were added).
- **"Returns Value $" is the marketplace's credit memos of the week** (user, 2026-11-24: "En Lowes y básicamente todos los marketplaces eso de returns value debe venir de lo de credit memos, pero arregla las descripciones"). The key stays `returns_value`, on the 13 rows that carry it (Home Depot Canada / USA, Lowe's, Rona, Wayfair Canada / USA, Sinks Direct Canada / USA, BB&B / Overstock, Best Buy, Walmart Canada / USA, Menards; the Amazon tab has no such row): Odoo's posted customer credit notes (`account.move`, `out_refund`) of the country's company whose own date falls in the column's data week, before tax, in the company's currency (`amount_untaxed_signed`: Wayfair Canada's notes are in USD and go at Odoo's rate of their day) — the very notes of the Operations tab's "Credit memos ($)", one marketplace's share of them, as the WEEK's total (no longer a 4-week average; my reading, said to him). Until that day it was the weekly average value of the goods Odoo received back over 4 weeks: the marketplaces credit what their customers return and keep most of the goods, so Home Depot USA read 66 USD a week where its credit memos are 2,600–4,600.
  - **A credit note says which marketplace it is for only by its customer.** The notes are typed by hand in Odoo — no order, no reversed invoice, no marketplace (`invoice_origin`, `reversed_entry_id`, `marketplace_name` are empty on all of them, and 1 line of 1,602 named a sale line) — and in Odoo a marketplace IS one customer: every order of the channel is invoiced to it (Lowe's USA, Home Depot, Home Depot USA, Rona Inc., WAYFAIR CANADA, WAYFAIR USA, SINKS DIRECT CANADA, Sinks Direct USA, Bed Bath and Beyond, Best Buy Canada, Walmart (Canada), Walmart USA, Menards Inc USA). `creditMemos` in `scorecard-fill` asks Odoo who the channel's orders of the last year are invoiced to (a customer with under 5 % of them — a warranty claim entered under the store — is not the channel's) and takes the notes made out to that customer in that company. Nothing is hard-coded: a tab found exactly its one customer on every one of the 13 rows.
  - **The whole note counts, not only its product lines**: Home Depot USA's and Menards' notes are a lump sum with no product, Home Depot Canada's carry its 2 % selling fee, Rona's a "Refunds Rona" line. So it is what the marketplace credited, as on the Operations tab.
  - **What it reads** (W44–W47, then the week in progress; before → after): Lowe's 2,046 / 3,769 / 4,507 / 3,907 → 3,101.94 / 3,283.88 / 2,859.45 / 1,988.51; Home Depot USA 50 / 66 / 66 / 66 → 4,152 / 2,599 / 4,646 / 3,444; Home Depot Canada 3,681 / 4,119 / 5,318 / 3,210 → 4,906.69 / 3,309.35 / 3,613.39 / 3,653.53; Rona 2,614 / 2,128 / 3,305 / 1,884 → 0 / 0 / 7,762.28 / 0 (Rona's notes are few and large). Sinks Direct USA, Walmart (both) and Menards read 0: Odoo has no credit note for them in those weeks (none at all for Sinks Direct USA and Walmart USA in 2026). Canada's tabs add up to the Operations tab's number: W47 = Home Depot 3,653.53 + Sinks Direct 1,064.40 + Wayfair 207.19 = 4,925.12.
  - **Not changed:** "Return Rate %" (`return_rate`: orders with a return Odoo RECEIVED over 4 weeks ÷ the orders placed — a credit note names no order, so it cannot be worked out from them; asked of him) and BB&B's "Return Actions Taken" note (what came back to the warehouse, by SKU). A row's description is its key's `label` in autoMetrics.js (the tooltip's "Filled by the PIM: …"); a PIM row's Source field is not shown.
  - **Deployed 2026-11-24 at his "Sí, despliega"** (`scorecard-fill` **v47**, 11 files, `verify_jwt` off as it was; the code went to main first, 1ba5428, at his "Commit y push a main" — auto mode had refused the deploy on that word alone). Checked before it with the function's own file run on the dev machine in preview mode, every write refused there (446 reads, nothing tried to write): 43 of the 65 Returns Value cells change, each week's number added up by hand against Odoo's notes for Lowe's and both Home Depots; every other key reads as the scorecard held it (`return_rate` 63 of 63 the same; the only other differences were the store's own rows, which that machine cannot read, and an order that came in during the run). After it: a preview through the deployed function gave the same numbers, then one fill per tab (`{ mode: "fill", boards: [tab], backfill: 3, refresh: 3, running: true }` on the nine tabs, 632 values written) rewrote W44 on, and the 13 rows read back from the database hold the function's numbers on 65 of 65 cells. The page was not opened in a browser.
- **Since 2026-11-09 this note is built straight from the data, without AI, in blocks** (user: it read as one messy paragraph): per SKU the drop, units against usual, stock, promotion, cause and To do, then Recorded in the PIM; `NoteBody` in ScorecardTable.jsx shows each block as a card. What follows is how it started.
- **"Actions Taken for top loosers" is written by AI** (user, 2026-11-05: "Automática" — a PIM row, read-only; key `losers_note`, unit text, this week's column only): the tab's top 5 losers (sales of the data week under the SKU's weekly average of the 8 weeks before, average ≥ $50), what the PIM's data shows for each (our warehouse stock and ETA, the store page from `where_to_buy_readings` — `pages` per board — sold out / broken / live) and the actions the PIM recorded that week for that channel (promotions created on its `promo` channels, Activity Log entries on those channels — a Wix push only for that store — and PIM edits of those SKUs; Where to Buy link edits left out). Gemini (`GEMINI_API_KEY`, gemini-2.5-flash, the description formatter's key) writes 2–4 sentences from those facts only; the only causes it may state are no stock, a sold-out page or a broken link (a price or a promotion is never a cause — it first read "$807 under a $917 MAP" as one). A text naming a SKU it was not given, leaving one out, over 700 characters, or saying "No action recorded" while actions exist (or the reverse) is thrown away for a plain sentence of the same facts. Written once per column (a PIM-written note is kept; a typed one is replaced, like any PIM row); `regenerate: true` rewrites it. Actions done outside the PIM (portal coupons, POs, calls) are not known to it. **A long note opens in full** (user, the same day: "que al darle click al icono … se abra en grande, para poder copiar"): its message icon is a button opening `NoteDialog` in ScorecardTable.jsx (the text, Copy, and Edit when the cell is typed by hand); it is portaled into `<main>` — inside the `<td>` the cell's tooltip covered the text, and in `<body>` its header became a second banner landmark (axe).
- **Cleanup of 2026-11-05** (user: "veo que hay redundancias"): duplicates archived (history kept) — BB&B "Total Sales" (SALES stays; Odoo cannot split BB&B from Overstock, Overstock has no orders of its own there), Wayfair USA "Sales USD" and "Total Sales. Thousands USD" (Sales stays; the thousands row stays on Canada, whose sales are CAD), Sinks Direct "Return no received $" (the "$ of returns not received" row stays), and the ads twins "Ads Sales" / "Ads Sales cad" / "Acos%" where "Total Ads Sales" / "Ads ACoS" exist; typos in row names fixed ("Actiosn … loosers", "Inevntory", "retunrs", "Cupons", "Obtain", "Shipping Que", "# SKU not Sales" → "# SKU not sold"…) — a typo had hidden "$ of returns not received" from its theme (sections are read from the name), it moved to Returns & refunds. **One source per PIM row**: the 14 weeks of every server-filled row were recomputed with `replaceTyped: true` (348 typed / imported values replaced, each copied into that board's Activity Log entry, metadata `replaced`), so a row's trend is the PIM's from W31 on; rows the PIM can only read today (stock, obsoletes, reviews, profitability, the AI note) keep their typed history. Targets: the user sets them by hand (few rows have one, so few cells are green / red).
- **The scorecard starts at W44** (user, 2026-11-05: "empezar todo desde W44, ya que es desde donde se recolectará la data" — hide, not delete): `FIRST_WEEK` in [weeks.js](src/features/scorecard/lib/weeks.js) (Sunday 2026-11-01, the numbers of Oct 25–31); the page shows no column before it — until 13 weeks have passed the grid is W44 and the 12 weeks after it, the coming ones empty and not editable (one column alone stretched across the page: "se ve horrible"), then it slides as before; the "earlier" arrow is off at W44, the table opens scrolled to the week in progress (`th[data-running]`), and the summary strip reads this week, never a coming one. scorecard-fill writes no column before it (`FIRST_COLUMN`). The workbook's history and the PIM's backfill stay in the database, hidden.
- **The week in progress fills itself** (user, 2026-11-06: "que la semana que va corriendo se vayan actualizando los números", every 3–4 hours): its column is next Sunday's (`thisWeek + 7`; on Friday Nov 6, W45 = Nov 1–7), and the grid ends on it. `scorecard-fill` `{ running: true }` adds that column, `{ running: "only" }` does only it (~10 s per tab), always replaced; read-today rows (stock, obsoletes, reviews, profitability) and the AI note stay on this week's column. Cron `scorecard-fill-running` (**every hour since 2026-11-22**, `15 * * * *`, at the user's word; until then `15 1,13,16,19,22 * * *` UTC; one request per tab) and the daily run. **No run between midnight Eastern and the labels' sync (09:30 UTC):** an order's shipping deadline ends at midnight and its label is known only after that sync, so a run there would read it as late. (No row reads the labels since 2026-11-17, so the hourly schedule of 2026-11-22 runs through those hours too.) MAP %, promotions, deals and coupons (`LIVE_KEYS` in [_shared/scorecardLive.js](supabase/functions/_shared/scorecardLive.js)) go in the same runs, on this week's column (daily run) and the running one (every run), and again when an editor opens the tab. MAP % is the very report Pricing shows: its code is [_shared/priceAlignmentReport.js](supabase/functions/_shared/priceAlignmentReport.js), used by the app (`expectedPrices.js`, `priceAlignment.js`) and the server; after changing it, redeploy `scorecard-fill` with its shared files. Typed rows stay closed until the week ends. Shown in italics with no target color (mid-week sales would always miss), the cell's tooltip "So far: Nov 1 – Nov 6 · updated Fri 9:50 AM". On Sunday the column becomes this week's and the daily run replaces it with the whole week. Checked the same day (live data, writes blocked): 1920 / 1440 / 390 in both themes, axe 0, the column in view on opening, the document never scrolls.
- **BB&B / Overstock's profitability, returns and reviews** (user, 2026-11-09): `no_profit_skus` there = products whose price paid this week (`wholesale` in BOARDS: WC Purple while a deal on that channel has the SKU, Orange in the monthly promotion, else Blue) is under Odoo's average cost; the marketplace pays the shipping (its ShipStation labels are $0), so none is added. `returns_note` lists what Odoo received back in the data week. `reviews_new` = what each store listing gained over the column's data week (its count when the column opened against when the one before opened): `where_to_buy_links.review_count` (the page's JSON-LD, [20261109_where_to_buy_review_count.sql](supabase/migrations/20261109_where_to_buy_review_count.sql)), counts per listing in `app_settings.scorecard_review_counts`; a listing seen for the first time adds nothing. Inventory Age, Buy Box Loss and Vine were archived on this tab (they do not apply).
- **BB&B / Overstock has "Top Losers Sales"** (user, 2026-11-09; key `top_losers_sales`): the Odoo sales (products only, no shipping) of the user's list of SKUs (61 on 2026-11-09), kept in `app_settings.scorecard_top_losers` (`{ "bbb_overstock": [SKUs] }`, change the list there).
- **Owners were removed from every measurable** (user, 2026-11-05: "después se los asigno yo manualmente"; 196 rows, the list before in the Activity Log entry's metadata): until they are assigned again only admins type the manual rows.
- **ShipStation is out of the ScoreCard** (user, 2026-11-17, after asking whether it was already disconnected and being told which rows it fed: "Desconecta ShipStation de EOS ScoreCard más nada, de cualquier item o KPI, pero solo de EOS ScoreCard"). No number of the ScoreCard comes from ShipStation's labels any more: `scorecard-fill` and `_shared/scorecardOperations.ts` do not read `shipstation_labels`. Nothing else was disconnected: `shipstation-labels-sync`, its daily cron and the table go on as before (the assistant can still read the table).
  - **Typed by their people since that day** — 48 measurables, whose keys are gone from `AUTO_KEYS`, `SERVER_KEYS` and `OPS_KEYS` (the pencil no longer offers them): On-Time Shipping % and Late Shipping Rate % on every marketplace tab (30), and on Operations (18) Cost per label, Orders processed, Return label cost, Wayfair: same-day shipping, Processed on time, Not processed in 24 h and each marketplace's on-time shipping (the copies of the marketplace tabs' rows). The weeks they had keep their numbers, with the PIM's dot (locked for everyone since 2026-11-23, like every number the PIM wrote); the 39 numbers of the week in progress were removed (a part of a week nobody would complete). One Activity Log entry ("EOS ScoreCard · ShipStation disconnected…") keeps each row's key and the numbers removed: putting them back starts from that list. Operations: 37 of its 88 rows are the PIM's.
  - **"# SKUs with No Profitability" on Sinks Direct stays the PIM's, on the cost alone**: this week's price under Odoo's average cost. The shipping cost per unit came from the labels, and Odoo keeps none. That day it gave the same numbers (Canada 1: A-909 at 30 under a cost of 30.16; USA 0). BB&B / Overstock's never added shipping.
  - **The Company tab's On-time shipping** averages the marketplace tabs' "On-Time Shipping %" rows as before, found by their NAME (`ON_TIME_ROW` in company.js: `loadCompany` reads them with `ilike 'on-time shipping%'` on the marketplace boards and marks them `role`) — a typed row has no key. A tab whose row is renamed leaves the average. Its three targets (at least 96 %) stay; the weeks ahead are empty until the tabs' rows are typed.
  - Odoo's own delivery date could date a shipment instead (set aside on 2026-11-05: a batch of June–August deliveries was validated in Odoo on Oct 16): said to the user, not built.
  - **Deployed 2026-11-23 (`scorecard-fill` v46, at the user's "Sí, despliega", with the Operations tab's credit memos):** every tab read in preview mode before and after — 797 numbers the same, 2 of WeCare's live ones moved, none gone. **State 2026-11-17, until that deploy: the rows and the app's code were changed and the function was not deployed** — auto mode refused the deploy, which waited for the user's word. The function deployed (v45) finds no row with those keys, so it fills none of them; until the deploy it still adds ShipStation's shipping cost to Sinks Direct's profitability (the same numbers that day). Checked: a preview of every tab before and after (482 numbers the same, the 14 keys gone, nothing else changed but two of WeCare's live numbers); the new function's own file run on the dev machine in preview mode, every write refused (475 the same — the store's own rows read empty there, the machine has no Wix key); the Company view built with the app's own code from live rows (15 on-time rows found by name, W44–W46 as before).
- **ShipStation's labels are copied into the PIM** (2026-11-05; no row of the ScoreCard reads them since 2026-11-17 — the bullet above, [20261105_shipstation_labels.sql](supabase/migrations/20261105_shipstation_labels.sql)): `shipstation_labels` (label id, account us / ca, tracking number, created_at = when the label was made, ship date, cost, currency, carrier, service, voided, return), written by the edge function `shipstation-labels-sync` (`SHIPSTATION_API_KEY` = the USA account, `SHIPSTATION_CA_API_KEY` = the Canada account; modes `sync` — from 3 days before the newest stored label —, `backfill` `{ account, from, to }` and `status`; cron `shipstation-labels-sync-daily` 09:30 UTC, before the scorecard; `verify_jwt` off, cron secret / service role / admin). The v2 API is slow — ~45 s for a week of the Canada account (~450 labels, every channel), so a backfill goes a week per call; loaded from 2026-05-01. Read only by admins in the app (RLS). Labels match Odoo's deliveries by `carrier_tracking_ref` (comma-separated for several packages). The two Amazon-only rows on the Sinks Direct board (Vine reviews, Products in prime) were archived. Left typed: the 9–10 Google Ads rows (need Google Ads API access) and "Actions Taken for top loosers" (text). Cron `scorecard-fill-daily` (10:00 and 11:00 UTC = 6 AM Toronto in either season; `{ mode: "fill", backfill: 8, refresh: 8 }`, pg_net timeout 150 s — a run takes ~30 s, mostly the US reviews) replaces this week's column and the PIM's own values of the 8 weeks before, so Odoo orders imported late, refunds and returns still land; a typed or imported value is never replaced, and a past column the PIM never wrote is written only where empty. Loaded 2026-11-05 for W31–W44 (Aug 2 – Nov 1, 144 values; checked by hand against Odoo for Oct 18–24: $9,359.88, 20 orders, 30 units). Test with `{ mode: "preview", backfill: N }` (nothing written; it also returns `diagnostics` — refunds read, refunds never shipped, returns, reviews by origin, the SKUs below cost); the last run's report is `app_settings.scorecard_fill`. Toronto midnights are cached per day (`etMidnightUtc`): computed per order line, a 14-week run died of WORKER_RESOURCE_LIMIT.
- **"Goal" is "Target" on the whole page, and a row's Target is set from its cell** (user, 2026-11-12: "en vez de goal debe llamarse target en todo el EOS", "que permita hacer cambios para los goal"). The column, the week's strip (on target / off target / without a target), the pencil's "Weekly target" and every text say Target; the stored columns keep their first names, `goal` and `goal_op`, and [format.js](src/features/scorecard/lib/format.js) speaks of targets (`TARGET_OPS`, `meetsTarget`, `targetLabel`, `targetText`, `parseTarget`, `targetInput`).
  - **The Target cell is a button for an admin** (`canEditTarget` / `onEditTarget` on ScorecardTable; not on a text row): [TargetDialog](src/features/scorecard/components/TargetDialog.jsx) — the comparison (at least / more than / at most / less than) and the number, which takes the focus; Enter saves. "95", "95%" and "$80,000" are read; a comparison typed with the number ("> 80") is refused, never guessed. An empty number removes the target: the row is tracked without color. `setMetricTarget` writes it, with one Activity Log entry that carries the target before ("… target: at least 95% (it was no target)"). The PIM's rows take a target like any other — only their numbers are locked. The pencil keeps its own target field, read by the same `parseTarget`.
  - A target is one value per row: changing it colors the past weeks again.
  - **On / off target is the number's own color over a faint fill of its cell, with no check and no cross** (user, 2026-11-22, with the team's word about the marks — "esa x roja y chulito verde no estaban antes, es mejor sin eso": "Haremos verde y roja la celda, algo tenue pero que se note"). In ScorecardTable's `ValueCell`: `text-success` / `text-error` on the number and `bg-success-tint` / `bg-error-tint` on the cell — two tokens of their own in index.css, one value per theme (light #e6f1e9 / #fde6e4, dark #142c1c / #3a1513; the number on its fill is 5.2:1 at the least in the light theme, 8.9:1 in the dark one). A judged cell takes that fill in the column of the week under review too; any other cell keeps only that column's tint. With the marks gone the eye gets color alone, so the cell's tooltip ("On target" / "Off target") and its screen-reader text say it in words. The week strip's counters keep their check and cross.
    - What came before, so it is not tried again: the full container fills until 2026-11-12 (user, once most rows had a target: "muy chillones e invasivos"), then the colored number with its check / cross and no fill; a pill around the number still read as a wall of pink; coloring only the misses read as two different rules.
    - Checked 2026-11-22 in a browser with a stand-in sign-in, live reads and every write kept in the test: the Company tab and Amazon at 1440, the Company tab at 390, both themes — no mark left in a judged cell, axe's only finding the top bar's account button, no sideways scroll, the document never scrolls.
  - **The week's counters are the filters of the grid** (user, the same day: "filtrar si quiere ver los on track, los off track o off target"): [WeekStrip](src/features/scorecard/components/WeekStrip.jsx) — "N on target", "N off target" and "N without a target" are buttons (`aria-pressed`). Pressed, the grid shows only those measurables, by the numbers of the week the strip names (the latest closed one shown); pressed again, or "Show all", every row. A counter at 0 is left out unless it is the pressed one, and where the filter keeps no row the grid's place says so, with "Show every measurable". The filter is in the address (`?show=on|off|none`; [status.js](src/features/scorecard/lib/status.js): `statusOf`, `summarizeWeek`, `rowsWithStatus`, `judgedWeek`), so it stays while the marketplaces are walked one by one, like Price Alignment's Above / Below. A row with a target and no number that week is in none of the three.
  - **"Left to fill" is the first counter** (user, 2026-11-16: "filtrar por los KPI que están en blank para que ellos puedan ir llenándolos más fácil"; `?show=empty`, `isToFill` in status.js): the measurables a person types — never one of the PIM's, text rows included — that have nothing in the week the strip names. Pressed, the grid is that list; a number typed there stays in it, to be read back and corrected, while the counter goes down, and leaves when the filter, the tab or the country changes (`justFilled` in Scorecard.jsx). A tab with nothing to type shows no counter. On the way: the grid's box takes the keyboard's focus while it scrolls sideways (`tabIndex={0}`: a viewer's grid, or a filtered one, can hold no button — axe `scrollable-region-focusable`). Checked that day in a browser with a stand-in sign-in, live reads and every write captured (Amazon, Wayfair, Sinks Direct, Best Buy, the department's tab, the company view; an admin and a viewer) at 1440 / 390 in both themes: axe's only finding the top bar's account button, no sideways scroll.
  - The dialog's form sits inside the dialog and Save names it (`form=`): a `<form>` that is itself the dialog (`as="form"`) is a role a form may not take (axe `aria-allowed-role`). The pencil's dialog and the app's other `as="form"` dialogs still draw that finding.
- **Company tab** (user, 2026-11-11: "una tab nueva que solo vería Yenny, Pedro y Vladimir … un total / top view de toda la empresa"; the full list of rows was still to come — "ve construyendo mientras tanto"). `?board=company`, the first tab, drawn by the same grid ([CompanyBoard](src/features/scorecard/components/CompanyBoard.jsx) → ScorecardTable with its own `groups`, `plain`).
  - **Who sees it:** the emails in `app_settings.scorecard_company_viewers` (Yenny, Pedro, Vladimir — and Jose, added so he could review it: ask before leaving him). Nobody else gets the button, its address shows them the first marketplace, and RLS refuses them the tab's own table (`scorecard_company_viewer()`, [20261111_scorecard_company.sql](supabase/migrations/20261111_scorecard_company.sql)). The marketplace tabs it adds up stay open to everyone, as before.
